{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nGRPO RLVR Recovery Training for pruned models.\\n\\nUses trl.GRPOTrainer with GSM8K answer-matching reward to recover\\nreasoning ability of structurally pruned models.\\n\\nv4: reward desert detection, disk-friendly checkpointing, max_completion_length=4096\\nv5: low-temperature sampling + repetition_penalty to combat degenerate repetition\\n    (--temperature, --repetition_penalty, --top_p, --top_k)\\n    --sampling_quality_check: pre-training sanity check on generation quality\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport random\\nimport re\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, TrainerCallback\\nfrom model_utils import load_pruned_model\\nfrom trl import GRPOConfig, GRPOTrainer\\n\\ntry:\\n    import wandb\\nexcept ImportError:\\n    wandb = None\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n)\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\ndef set_seed(seed: int):\\n    random.seed(seed)\\n    np.random.seed(seed)\\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\\n\\n\\nclass RewardDesertCallback(TrainerCallback):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Stop training if reward stays at zero for too many consecutive log events.\\n\\n    v2 lesson: pruned model too weak -> reward=0 every sample -> loss=0,\\n    grad_norm=0, zero learning signal for hundreds of steps.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, patience: int = 50):\\n        self.patience = patience\\n        self.zero_reward_count = 0\\n        self.last_logged_step = -1\\n\\n    def on_log(self, args, state, control, logs=None, **kwargs):\\n        if logs is None:\\n            return\\n        # trl GRPOTrainer logs reward under various keys depending on version\\n        reward_mean = logs.get(\\\"reward\\\", logs.get(\\\"rewards/mean\\\", None))\\n        if reward_mean is None:\\n            return\\n        if state.global_step == self.last_logged_step:\\n            return\\n        self.last_logged_step = state.global_step\\n\\n        if reward_mean == 0.0:\\n            self.zero_reward_count += 1\\n            if self.zero_reward_count >= self.patience:\\n                logger.warning(\\n                    f\\\"REWARD DESERT: reward=0 for {self.zero_reward_count} consecutive \\\"\\n                    f\\\"log events (~{self.zero_reward_count * args.logging_steps} steps). \\\"\\n                    f\\\"Pruned model may be too weak for GRPO. Stopping.\\\"\\n                )\\n                control.should_training_stop = True\\n        else:\\n            self.zero_reward_count = 0\\n\\n\\ndef extract_answer(text: str) -> str | None:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract numeric answer from model output.\\n    Tries \\\\\\\\boxed{} first, then last number in text.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # Try \\\\boxed{...}\\n    boxed = re.findall(r\\\"\\\\\\\\boxed\\\\{([^}]+)\\\\}\\\", text)\\n    if boxed:\\n        return boxed[-1].strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n\\n    # Try #### format (GSM8K style)\\n    hash_match = re.findall(r\\\"####\\\\s*(.+)\\\", text)\\n    if hash_match:\\n        return hash_match[-1].strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n\\n    # Fall back to last number\\n    numbers = re.findall(r\\\"-?\\\\d+(?:,\\\\d{3})*(?:\\\\.\\\\d+)?\\\", text)\\n    if numbers:\\n        return numbers[-1].replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    return None\\n\\n\\ndef normalize_answer(ans: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Normalize a numeric answer string for comparison.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ans = ans.strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    try:\\n        val = float(ans)\\n        if val == int(val):\\n            return str(int(val))\\n        return str(val)\\n    except (ValueError, OverflowError):\\n        return ans\\n\\n\\ndef extract_gold_answer(answer_text: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract gold answer from GSM8K answer field (after ####).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    match = re.search(r\\\"####\\\\s*(.+)\\\", answer_text)\\n    if match:\\n        return normalize_answer(match.group(1))\\n    return answer_text.strip()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.16}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nGRPO RLVR Recovery Training for pruned models.\\n\\nUses trl.GRPOTrainer with GSM8K answer-matching reward to recover\\nreasoning ability of structurally pruned models.\\n\\nv4: reward desert detection, disk-friendly checkpointing, max_completion_length=4096\\nv5: low-temperature sampling + repetition_penalty to combat degenerate repetition\\n    (--temperature, --repetition_penalty, --top_p, --top_k)\\n    --sampling_quality_check: pre-training sanity check on generation quality\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport random\\nimport re\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, TrainerCallback\\nfrom model_utils import load_pruned_model\\nfrom trl import GRPOConfig, GRPOTrainer\\n\\ntry:\\n    import wandb\\nexcept ImportError:\\n    wandb = None\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n)\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\ndef set_seed(seed: int):\\n    random.seed(seed)\\n    np.random.seed(seed)\\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\n    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\\n\\n\\nclass RewardDesertCallback(TrainerCallback):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Stop training if reward stays at zero for too many consecutive log events.\\n\\n    v2 lesson: pruned model too weak -> reward=0 every sample -> loss=0,\\n    grad_norm=0, zero learning signal for hundreds of steps.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, patience: int = 50):\\n        self.patience = patience\\n        self.zero_reward_count = 0\\n        self.last_logged_step = -1\\n\\n    def on_log(self, args, state, control, logs=None, **kwargs):\\n        if logs is None:\\n            return\\n        # trl GRPOTrainer logs reward under various keys depending on version\\n        reward_mean = logs.get(\\\"reward\\\", logs.get(\\\"rewards/mean\\\", None))\\n        if reward_mean is None:\\n            return\\n        if state.global_step == self.last_logged_step:\\n            return\\n        self.last_logged_step = state.global_step\\n\\n        if reward_mean == 0.0:\\n            self.zero_reward_count += 1\\n            if self.zero_reward_count >= self.patience:\\n                logger.warning(\\n                    f\\\"REWARD DESERT: reward=0 for {self.zero_reward_count} consecutive \\\"\\n                    f\\\"log events (~{self.zero_reward_count * args.logging_steps} steps). \\\"\\n                    f\\\"Pruned model may be too weak for GRPO. Stopping.\\\"\\n                )\\n                control.should_training_stop = True\\n        else:\\n            self.zero_reward_count = 0\\n\\n\\ndef extract_answer(text: str) -> str | None:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract numeric answer from model output.\\n    Tries \\\\\\\\boxed{} first, then last number in text.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # Try \\\\boxed{...}\\n    boxed = re.findall(r\\\"\\\\\\\\boxed\\\\{([^}]+)\\\\}\\\", text)\\n    if boxed:\\n        return boxed[-1].strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n\\n    # Try #### format (GSM8K style)\\n    hash_match = re.findall(r\\\"####\\\\s*(.+)\\\", text)\\n    if hash_match:\\n        return hash_match[-1].strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n\\n    # Fall back to last number\\n    numbers = re.findall(r\\\"-?\\\\d+(?:,\\\\d{3})*(?:\\\\.\\\\d+)?\\\", text)\\n    if numbers:\\n        return numbers[-1].replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    return None\\n\\n\\ndef normalize_answer(ans: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Normalize a numeric answer string for comparison.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ans = ans.strip().replace(\\\",\\\", \\\"\\\").replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    try:\\n        val = float(ans)\\n        if val == int(val):\\n            return str(int(val))\\n        return str(val)\\n    except (ValueError, OverflowError):\\n        return ans\\n\\n\\ndef extract_gold_answer(answer_text: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract gold answer from GSM8K answer field (after ####).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    match = re.search(r\\\"####\\\\s*(.+)\\\", answer_text)\\n    if match:\\n        return normalize_answer(match.group(1))\\n    return answer_text.strip()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.16}}"
